return }\r\n        setOrder((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, ...result.data }))\r\n        addToast(`C─ÉT ─æ├ú duyß╗çt b├ío gi├í ${order.name}`)\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    async function handleReject() {\r\n        const result = await rejectQuotation(order.id, rejectReason)\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        setOrder((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, ...result.data }))\r\n        setShowRejectDialog(false)\r\n        addToast(`B├ío gi├í ${order.name} ─æ├ú bß╗ï tß╗½ chß╗æi`)\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    async function handleConvertToContract() {\r\n        if (!confirm(`Chuyß╗ân b├ío gi├í \"${order.name}\" th├ánh Hß╗úp ─æß╗ông ─æ├ám ph├ín?`)) return\r\n        const result = await convertToContract(order.id)\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        addToast(`─É├ú tß║ío hß╗úp ─æß╗ông ${result.data.name}`)\r\n        router.push(`/sale/${result.data.id}`)\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // ΓöÇΓöÇ Contract actions ΓöÇΓöÇ\r\n    async function handleSign() {\r\n        if (!confirm(`K├╜ hß╗úp ─æß╗ông \"${order.name}\"?`)) return\r\n        const result = await signContract(order.id)\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        setOrder((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, ...result.data }))\r\n        addToast(`─É├ú k├╜ hß╗úp ─æß╗ông ${order.name}`)\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    async function handleDone() {\r\n        const result = await updateOrderState(order.id, 'DONE')\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        setOrder((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, ...result.data }))\r\n        addToast(`─É├ú ho├án th├ánh ${order.name}`)\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // ΓöÇΓöÇ Generic actions ΓöÇΓöÇ\r\n    async function handleStateChange(nextState: string) {\r\n        const result = await updateOrderState(order.id, nextState)\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        setOrder((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, ...result.data }))\r\n        addToast(`─É├ú cß║¡p nhß║¡t trß║íng th├íi`)\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    async function handleSaveLines() {\r\n        setSaving(true)\r\n        const result = await saveOrderLines(order.id, lines as any)\r\n        setSaving(false)\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        setOrder((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, totalAmount }))\r\n        setEditingLines(false)\r\n        addToast('─É├ú cß║¡p nhß║¡t dß╗ïch vß╗Ñ')\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    async function handleSaveMS() {\r\n        setSaving(true)\r\n        const result = await saveMilestones(order.id, milestones as any)\r\n        setSaving(false)\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        setEditingMS(false)\r\n        addToast('─É├ú cß║¡p nhß║¡t milestones')\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    async function handleDelete() {\r\n        if (!confirm(`X├│a \"${order.name}\"? Tß║Ñt cß║ú dß╗ïch vß╗Ñ v├á milestones sß║╜ bß╗ï x├│a.`)) return\r\n        const result = await deleteOrder(order.id)\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        addToast('─É├ú x├│a'); router.push('/sale')\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    async function handleConvertToProject() {\r\n        if (!confirm(`Tß║ío dß╗▒ ├ín tß╗½ \"${order.name}\"?`)) return\r\n        const result = await convertOrderToProject(order.id)\r\n        if (!result.success) { addToast(result.error, 'error'); return }\r\n        addToast(`─É├ú tß║ío dß╗▒ ├ín ${result.data.code}`)\r\n        router.push(`/projects/${result.data.id}`)\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const totalPaid = milestones.filter(m => m.state === 'PAID').reduce((s, m) => s + Number(m.amount || 0), 0)\r\n    const paidPercent = Number(order.totalAmount) > 0 ? Math.round(totalPaid / Number(order.totalAmount) * 100) : 0\r\n\r\n    // ΓöÇΓöÇ Flow buttons based on docType + state ΓöÇΓöÇ\r\n    function renderFlowButtons() {\r\n        if (isQuotation) {\r\n            switch (order.state) {\r\n                case 'DRAFT':\r\n                    return (\r\n                        <>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-sm\" style={{ background: '#3B82F6', color: '#fff', border: 'none' }} onClick={handleSend}>≡ƒôñ Gß╗¡i C─ÉT</button>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" style={{ color: '#EF4444' }} onClick={() => handleStateChange('CANCEL')}>Huß╗╖</button>\r\n                        </>\r\n                    )\r\n                case 'SENT':\r\n                    return (\r\n                        <>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-sm\" style={{ background: '#22C55E', color: '#fff', border: 'none' }} onClick={handleApprove}>Γ£à C─ÉT duyß╗çt</button>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-sm\" style={{ background: '#EF4444', color: '#fff', border: 'none' }} onClick={() => setShowRejectDialog(true)}>Γ¥î Tß╗½ chß╗æi</button>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" onClick={() => handleStateChange('DRAFT')}>Γå⌐ Vß╗ü nh├íp</button>\r\n                        </>\r\n                    )\r\n                case 'APPROVED':\r\n                    return (\r\n                        <button className=\"btn btn-sm\" style={{ background: '#6366F1', color: '#fff', border: 'none' }} onClick={handleConvertToContract}>≡ƒô¥ Chuyß╗ân sang Hß╗úp ─æß╗ông</button>\r\n                    )\r\n                case 'REJECTED': case 'EXPIRED': case 'CANCEL':\r\n                    return (\r\n                        <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" onClick={() => handleStateChange('DRAFT')}>Γå⌐ Mß╗ƒ lß║íi</button>\r\n                    )\r\n                default: return null\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        if (isContract) {\r\n            switch (order.state) {\r\n                case 'NEGOTIATING':\r\n                    return (\r\n                        <>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-sm\" style={{ background: '#22C55E', color: '#fff', border: 'none' }} onClick={handleSign}>Γ£à K├╜ hß╗úp ─æß╗ông</button>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" style={{ color: '#EF4444' }} onClick={() => handleStateChange('CANCEL')}>Huß╗╖</button>\r\n                        </>\r\n                    )\r\n                case 'SIGNED':\r\n                    return (\r\n                        <>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-sm\" style={{ background: '#6366F1', color: '#fff', border: 'none' }} onClick={handleDone}>≡ƒÅü Ho├án th├ánh</button>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-accent btn-sm\" onClick={handleConvertToProject}>≡ƒÅù∩╕Å Tß║ío dß╗▒ ├ín</button>\r\n                        </>\r\n                    )\r\n                case 'CANCEL':\r\n                    return <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" onClick={() => handleStateChange('NEGOTIATING')}>Γå⌐ Mß╗ƒ lß║íi</button>\r\n                default: return null\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        return null\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    return (\r\n        <>\r\n            <ToastContainer toasts={toasts} />\r\n\r\n            {/* Reject Dialog */}\r\n            {showRejectDialog && (\r\n                <div style={{ position: 'fixed', inset: 0, background: 'rgba(0,0,0,.5)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', zIndex: 1000 }}>\r\n                    <div style={{ background: '#fff', borderRadius: 16, padding: 28, width: 420, maxWidth: '90vw' }}>\r\n                        <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 16px', fontSize: 16 }}>L├╜ do tß╗½ chß╗æi</h3>\r\n                        <textarea className=\"form-input\" value={rejectReason} onChange={e => setRejectReason(e.target.value)}\r\n                            rows={3} placeholder=\"Nhß║¡p l├╜ do C─ÉT tß╗½ chß╗æi...\" style={{ width: '100%', resize: 'vertical' }} />\r\n                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, justifyContent: 'flex-end', marginTop: 16 }}>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" onClick={() => setShowRejectDialog(false)}>Huß╗╖</button>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-sm\" style={{ background: '#EF4444', color: '#fff', border: 'none' }} onClick={handleReject}>Tß╗½ chß╗æi</button>\r\n                        </div>\r\n                    </div>\r\n                </div>\r\n            )}\r\n\r\n            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginBottom: 16, fontSize: 13, color: '#8FA3BF' }}>\r\n                <Link href=\"/sale\" style={{ color: '#8FA3BF', textDecoration: 'none' }}>B├ío gi├í & H─É</Link>\r\n                <span>ΓÇ║</span>\r\n                <span style={{ color: '#0F1C2E', fontWeight: 600 }}>{order.name}</span>\r\n            </div>\r\n\r\n            {/* Header */}\r\n            <div className=\"page-header\" style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>\r\n                <div>\r\n                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, marginBottom: 6 }}>\r\n                        <h1 className=\"page-title\" style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>{order.name}</h1>\r\n                        <span className={`badge badge-${allStateColors[order.state] || 'muted'}`}>{allStateLabels[order.state] || order.state}</span>\r\n                        <span style={{ fontSize: 11, background: isQuotation ? '#EFF6FF' : '#F5F3FF', color: isQuotation ? '#3B82F6' : '#6366F1', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: 4, fontWeight: 700 }}>\r\n                            {isQuotation ? '≡ƒôï B├ío gi├í' : '≡ƒô¥ Hß╗úp ─æß╗ông'}\r\n                        </span>\r\n                    </div>\r\n                    <p className=\"page-subtitle\">{order.partnerName} ΓÇó Tß╗òng: {formatCurrency(Number(order.totalAmount))}</p>\r\n                </div>\r\n                <div className=\"page-actions\" style={{ flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>\r\n                    {renderFlowButtons()}\r\n                    <a href={`/api/pdf/${initOrder.id}`} target=\"_blank\" className=\"btn btn-outline btn-sm\" style={{ textDecoration: 'none' }}>≡ƒôä Xuß║Ñt PDF</a>\r\n                    <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" style={{ color: '#EF4444' }} onClick={handleDelete}>X├│a</button>\r\n                </div>\r\n            </div>\r\n\r\n            {/* Linked quotation info */}\r\n            {isContract && order.quotation && (\r\n                <div className=\"card\" style={{ padding: 16, marginBottom: 20, background: '#FAFBFC', border: '1.5px dashed #CBD5E1' }}>\r\n                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, fontSize: 13 }}>\r\n                        <span style={{ color: '#8FA3BF' }}>Tß╗½ b├ío gi├í:</span>\r\n                        <Link href={`/sale/${order.quotation.id}`} style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: '#3B82F6' }}>{order.quotation.name}</Link>\r\n                        <span style={{ color: '#8FA3BF' }}>ΓÇö {order.quotation.partnerName} ΓÇö {formatCurrency(Number(order.quotation.totalAmount))}</span>\r\n                    </div>\r\n                </div>\r\n            )}\r\n\r\n            {/* Payment Progress (only for contracts) */}\r\n            {isContract && (\r\n                <div className=\"card\" style={{ padding: 20, marginBottom: 20 }}>\r\n                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 12 }}>\r\n                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14 }}>Tiß║┐n ─æß╗Ö thanh to├ín</div>\r\n                        <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: '#22C55E' }}>{formatCurrency(totalPaid)} / {formatCurrency(Number(order.totalAmount))}</div>\r\n                    </div>\r\n                    <div className=\"progress\" style={{ height: 12, marginBottom: 8 }}>\r\n                        <div className=\"progress-bar\" style={{ width: `${paidPercent}%`, background: '#22C55E' }} />\r\n                    </div>\r\n                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: 12, color: '#8FA3BF' }}>\r\n                        <span>─É├ú thu: {paidPercent}%</span>\r\n                        <span>C├▓n lß║íi: {formatCurrency(Number(order.totalAmount) - totalPaid)}</span>\r\n                    </div>\r\n                </div>\r\n            )}\r\n\r\n            <div className=\"grid-2\" style={{ alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 20 }}>\r\n                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>\r\n                    {/* Info */}\r\n                    <div className=\"card\" style={{ padding: 20 }}>\r\n                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14, marginBottom: 14 }}>{isQuotation ? 'Th├┤ng tin b├ío gi├í' : 'Th├┤ng tin hß╗úp ─æß╗ông'}</div>\r\n                        {[\r\n                            { label: 'Kh├ích h├áng', value: order.partnerName },\r\n                            { label: 'Email', value: order.partnerEmail ?? 'ΓÇö' },\r\n                            { label: 'S─ÉT', value: order.partnerPhone ?? 'ΓÇö' },\r\n                            { label: 'Ng├áy tß║ío', value: order.createdAt ? formatDate(String(order.createdAt).split('T')[0]) : 'ΓÇö' },\r\n                            ...(isQuotation ? [\r\n                                { label: 'Hiß╗çu lß╗▒c', value: order.validityDate ? formatDate(String(order.validityDate).split('T')[0]) : 'ΓÇö' },\r\n                                ...(order.sentAt ? [{ label: 'Ng├áy gß╗¡i', value: formatDate(String(order.sentAt).split('T')[0]) }] : []),\r\n                                ...(order.approvedAt ? [{ label: 'Ng├áy duyß╗çt', value: formatDate(String(order.approvedAt).split('T')[0]) }] : []),\r\n                            ] : [\r\n                                ...(order.signedAt ? [{ label: 'Ng├áy k├╜', value: formatDate(String(order.signedAt).split('T')[0]) }] : []),\r\n                            ]),\r\n                        ].map(({ label, value }) => (\r\n                            <div key={label} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 8 }}>\r\n                                <span style={{ color: '#8FA3BF', width: 100, flexShrink: 0 }}>{label}</span>\r\n                                <span style={{ color: '#0F1C2E' }}>{value}</span>\r\n                            </div>\r\n                        ))}\r\n                        {order.rejectedReason && (\r\n                            <div style={{ marginTop: 8, padding: '8px 12px', background: '#FEF2F2', borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 13, color: '#DC2626' }}>\r\n                                <strong>L├╜ do tß╗½ chß╗æi:</strong> {order.rejectedReason}\r\n                            </div>\r\n                        )}\r\n                    </div>\r\n\r\n                    {/* Lines */}\r\n                    <div className=\"card\" style={{ padding: 20 }}>\r\n                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 14 }}>\r\n                            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14 }}>Chi tiß║┐t dß╗ïch vß╗Ñ</div>\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" onClick={() => { if (editingLines) handleSaveLines(); else setEditingLines(true) }}>\r\n                                {saving ? 'ΓÅ│' : editingLines ? '≡ƒÆ╛ L╞░u' : 'Γ£Å∩╕Å Sß╗¡a'}\r\n                            </button>\r\n                        </div>\r\n\r\n                        {editingLines ? { ...l, description: e.target.value } : l))} />\r\n                                        <input className=\"form-input\" type=\"number\" value={line.qty} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}\r\n                                            onChange={e => setLines(prev => prev.map((l, j) => j === i ? { ...l, unitPrice: +e.target.value || 0 } : l))} />\r\n                                        <button onClick={() => setLines(prev => prev.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}\r\n                                            style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#8FA3BF' }}>Γ£ò</button>\r\n                                    </div>\r\n                                ))}\r\n                                <button onClick={() => setLines(prev => [...prev, { description: '', qty: 1, unitPrice: 0 }])}\r\n                                    className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" style={{ border: '1.5px dashed #CBD5E1', width: 'fit-content' }}>+ Th├¬m d├▓ng</button>\r\n                                <div style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 16, color: '#1F3A5F', marginTop: 8 }}>\r\n                                    Tß╗òng: {formatCurrency(totalAmount)}\r\n                                </div>\r\n                            </div>\r\n                        ) : (\r\n                            <table className=\"data-table\" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>\r\n                                <thead><tr><th>M├┤ tß║ú</th><th style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>SL</th><th style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>─É╞ín gi├í</th><th style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>Th├ánh tiß╗ün</th></tr></thead>\r\n                                <tbody>\r\n                                    {(order.lines || []).length === 0 ? : (order.lines || []).map((l: any) => (\r\n                                        <tr key={l.id}>\r\n                                            <td style={{ fontWeight: 500 }}>{l.description}</td>\r\n                                            <td style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#8FA3BF' }}>{l.qty}</td>\r\n                                            <td style={{ '#1F3A5F' }}>{formatCurrency(Number(l.subtotal || 0))}</td>\r\n                                        </tr>\r\n                                    ))}\r\n                                </tbody>\r\n                                <tfoot><tr style={{ background: '#F8F9FB' }}>\r\n                                    <td colSpan={3} style={{ fontWeight: 700, textAlign: 'right' }}>Tß╗òng</td>\r\n                                    <td style={{ fontWeight: 800, textAlign: 'right', color: '#1F3A5F' }}>{formatCurrency(Number(order.totalAmount))}</td>\r\n                                </tr></tfoot>\r\n                            </table>\r\n                        )}\r\n                    </div>\r\n                </div>\r\n\r\n                {/* Right: Milestones (only for contracts) */}\r\n                <div className=\"card\" style={{ padding: 20 }}>\r\n                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>\r\n                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14 }}>Milestones</div>\r\n                        {isContract && (\r\n                            <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" 'ΓÅ│' : editingMS ? '≡ƒÆ╛ L╞░u' : 'Γ£Å∩╕Å Sß╗¡a'}\r\n                            </button>\r\n                        )}\r\n                    </div>\r\n\r\n                    {editingMS ? (\r\n                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>\r\n                            {milestones.map((ms, i) => (\r\n                                <div key={i} style={{ border: '1.5px solid #E2E8F0', borderRadius: 10, padding: 14 }}>\r\n                                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}>\r\n                                        <input className=\"form-input\" value={ms.name} placeholder=\"T├¬n milestone\"\r\n                                            onChange={e => setMilestones(prev => prev.map((m, j) => j === i ? { ...m, name: e.target.value } : m))} style={{ flex: 1 }} />\r\n                                        <button onClick={() => setMilestones(prev => prev.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}\r\n                                            style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#EF4444' }}>Γ£ò</button>\r\n                                    </div>\r\n                                    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '80px 1fr', gap: 8 }}>\r\n                                        <input className=\"form-input\" type=\"number\" value={ms.percent} placeholder=\"%\"\r\n                                            onChange={e => setMilestones(prev => prev.map((m, j) => j === i ? }\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    await logAudit({ userId: user.id, action: 'convert', entity: 'sale_order', entityId: quotationId, details: `Quotation ΓåÆ Contract ${contract.id}` })\r\n    return Promise<ActionResult<Record<string, unknown>>> {\r\n    const user = await requirePermission('sale.edit')\r\n    const extra: Record<string, unknown> = { state, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() }\r\n    if (state === 'SIGNED') extra.signedAt = new Date().toISOString()\r\n    if (state === 'SENT') extra.sentAt = new Date().toISOString()\r\n    if (state === 'APPROVED') extra.approvedAt = new ΓöÇΓöÇ\r\nexport async function saveOrderLines(orderId: string, lines: OrderLineInput[]): Promise<ActionResult<void>> {\r\n    const user = await requirePermission('sale.edit')\r\n\r\n    // Validate each line\r\n    for (const line of lines) {\r\n        const parsed = parseInput(orderLineSchema, line)\r\n        if (!parsed.success) return fail(`D├▓ng \"${line.description || ''}\": i) => ({\r\n            orderId,\r\n            description: l.description,\r\n            qty: l.qty || 1,\r\n            unitPrice: l.unitPrice || 0,\r\n            subtotal: (l.qty || 1) * (l.unitPrice || 0),\r\n            sequence: i,\r\n        }))\r\n        const { error } = await supabase.from('sale_order_lines').insert(rows as any)\r\n        if (error) return fail(error.message)\r\n    }\r\n    const total = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.qty || 1) * (l.unitPrice || 0), 0)\r\n    await supabase.from('sale_orders').update({ totalAmount: total, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', orderId)\r\n\r\n    await logAudit({ userId: user.id, action: 'update', entity: 'sale_order_lines', entityId: orderId, details: `Cß║¡p nhß║¡t ${lines.length} d├▓ng
